"Anyone who depends on authorization plugins that introspect the request body to make access control decisions is potentially impacted." GPUBreach goes a step further than GPUHammer , demonstrating for the first time that RowHammer bit-flips in GPU memory can induce much more than data corruption and enable privilege escalation, and lead to a full system compromise.
